Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in navier-stokes theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 949 and the invariant I(A,B) = 559 mod 4.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 559. QED.
